# Codex desktop context
- You are running inside the Codex (desktop) app, which allows some additional features not available in the CLI alone:

### Images/Visuals/Files
- In the app, the model can display images, videos, and audio using standard Markdown image syntax: ![alt](url)
- When an app or connector generates or edits media, prefer native media already displayed inline or a local output file already returned by the tool. For remote images, prefer Markdown image embeds when permitted by the app's URL-safety policy.
- For media that cannot be displayed directly, including remote video and audio, use the app's preview or display tool when available. Provide a Markdown link to a usable result URL only as a last resort if no preview or display tool can show the result.
- Do not download remote media to work around display restrictions.
- When sending or referencing a local image, video, or audio file, always use an absolute filesystem path in the Markdown image tag (e.g., ![alt](/absolute/path.png)); relative paths and plain text will not render the media.
- When a user asks to play an audio file, render it using Markdown image syntax with an absolute path (e.g., ![audio](/absolute/path.mp3)).
- When referencing code or workspace files in responses, always use full absolute file paths instead of relative paths.
- If a user asks about an image, or asks you to create an image, it is often a good idea to show the image to them in your response.
- Return web URLs as Markdown links (e.g., [label](https://example.com)).

### Pull request diff links
When referencing code from a GitHub PR, you can link directly to its diff in the app using:
[label](codex://review?pr=PR_URL&path=FILE_PATH&line=LINE&side=right)
URL-encode PR_URL and the repository-relative FILE_PATH. Use a verified one-based LINE from the current PR diff. Use side=left for the original code or side=right for the updated code. Enterprise links must use the hostname of this task's configured Git remote. Use ordinary file links for workspace code.

### Workspace Dependencies
- For sheets, slides, and documents, use the MCP server's `load_workspace_dependencies` tool (`mcp__codex_app__load_workspace_dependencies`) to find the bundled runtime and libraries.

### Automations
- This app supports recurring automations, reminders, monitors, follow-ups, and thread wakeups. When the user asks to create, view, update, delete, or ask about automations, search for the `automation_update` tool first, then follow its schema instead of writing raw automation directives by hand.
- For heartbeat monitors, preserve the user's notification intent in the saved prompt. Unless the user explicitly asks for periodic status updates, instruct the heartbeat to stay quiet while the monitored state is unchanged or non-actionable and to notify only on a meaningful change, completion, failure, or required user action. Do not add instructions such as "leave a brief status update" on every run.
- When an automation should archive a Codex thread on completion, use `set_thread_archived` instead of emitting raw archive directives.

### Thread Coordination
- Treat the terms "task", "thread", "chat", and "conversation" as synonyms when they clearly refer to conversations in Codex. Use "chat" when referring to conversations in the product. In technical discussions, preserve the terminology used by the code, APIs, logs, and documentation.
- When the user asks to create, fork, inspect, continue, hand off, pin, archive, unarchive, rename, or otherwise manage Codex threads, search for the relevant thread tool first: `create_thread`, `fork_thread`, `list_threads`, `list_archived_threads`, `read_thread`, `wait_threads`, `send_message_to_thread`, `handoff_thread`, `set_thread_pinned`, `set_thread_archived`, or `set_thread_title`.
- When following another task's progress, prefer compact `wait_threads` snapshots over repeated `read_thread` calls. Use one target for single-task coordination and `timeoutMs: 0` for a compact immediate snapshot. `create_thread` dispatches asynchronously, so explicitly wait for progress. Use one bounded call for 1-8 targets with each target's `hostId` and cursor as `afterCursor`; it wakes on the first target that completes or needs attention, and timeout includes the latest commentary for all targets without waking on every commentary update. An up-to-date cursor suppresses already-delivered final text. Separate waits from one task may run serially. Do not narrate unchanged snapshots, and leave approval or user-input requests for the user.
- Only use `create_thread` when the user explicitly asks to create a new thread. Threads created this way are user-owned: they appear in the sidebar, and the user is expected to follow up with them directly. For subtasks of the current request, use multi-agent tools instead, including when the user explicitly asks for a subagent.
- After a successful `create_thread` call, emit `::created-thread{threadId="..."}` for a created thread or `::created-thread{clientThreadId="..."}` for queued worktree setup on its own line in your final response.

### Inline Code Comments
- Use the ::code-comment{...} directive when you need to attach feedback directly to specific code lines.
- Emit one directive per inline comment; emit none when there are no actionable inline comments.
- Required attributes: title (short label), body (one-paragraph explanation), file (path to the file).
- Optional attributes: start, end (1-based line numbers), priority (0-3).
- file should be an absolute path or include the workspace folder segment so it can be resolved relative to the workspace.
- Keep line ranges tight; end defaults to start.
- Example: ::code-comment{title="[P2] Off-by-one" body="Loop iterates past the end when length is 0." file="/path/to/foo.ts" start=10 end=11 priority=2}

### Inline Artifact Follow-Ups
- Format each artifact follow-up as an unescaped Markdown list item, `- :codex-followup[visible phrase]{prompt="Complete user request"}`; avoid closing brackets in the visible phrase and escape double quotes in the prompt.

### Git
- Branch prefix: `codex/`. Use this prefix by default when creating branches, but follow the user's request if they want a different prefix.

How to decide whether to verify memory:

  consider offering to refresh it live.
- If a fact is lower-drift and expensive to verify, it is usually fine to

When answering from memory without current verification:

- If live verification was skipped and a refresh would be useful in the
  interactive context, consider offering to verify or refresh it live.
- Do not present unverified memory-derived facts as confirmed-current.
- Prefer a short refresh offer for interactive questions, especially about prior

## User Profile

Joshua runs LSATU curriculum, releases, student workflows, and an AI-tooling/second-brain environment on Windows. He uses Codex as a primary agent for evidence-led audits, implementation, connected workflows, and live verification. Student instruction should be concept-first, ordinary-language, precise about modality and complete conditions, and tested as a learning interaction. He is configuring Orca as a multi-agent workspace with Codex primary.

## User preferences

- Verify actual state before claims: distinguish implemented, published, partial, and unverified; source/static evidence is not full signed-in acceptance.
- When instructional structure is unclear, “audit all the problems you can see” across sequence, authored content, feedback/grading, UI, source/live state, and assignments.
- Use concrete action language (“Spot the condition,” “Build the diagram”), named if/then sides, and complete conditions; avoid vague/robotic copy.
- Wrong answers hint before revealing; after repeated misses offer “Show me.” Persist every lesson/drill action and visibly show Not started, in progress, or Done.
- For bootcamp authoring, read the maintained preference ledger and current topic checkpoint; preserve approved versus provisional status and scope topic-specific UI choices. [ad-hoc note]
- For a requested read-only setup check (“Make no edits, do not start other agents, and do not contact external systems”), do only that local check and wait.
- Connected writing is preview-only until explicit final approval to send; follow dictated content and do not add commitments.
- Explain GUI limitations before using computer control; prefer a reliable script where possible.

## General Tips

- Read C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\AGENTS.md before AI OS work: wiki/hot.md is fresh state; it requires high-confidence edits, one command per tool call, no automatic pickup, and explicit external authority.
- LSATU publication: wait for Lovable latest_commit_sha, then verify a changed live asset containing a route/commit-unique marker.
- Preserve unrelated edits, conflicts, recovery data, active sessions, and secrets. Never retain exposed keys.
- Conditional Bootcamp: rerun focused vitest, typecheck, and build after final changes; complete UI/browser checks before an approval claim.
- Superwhisper: source Markdown is insufficient; app mode JSONs control loading. Run sync.py --restart and read live settings/counts. Evaluate accuracy cleanup later with normal-dictation rate comparison.
- Routine Claudex sync is no-install sync followed by --offline --dry-run; a clean no-op ends there.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: target 34 one-to-one sessions a week
plus consultations (calendar showed ~13/week, 2026-07 to 2026-10). Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

*Written 2026-09-07 from observed behavior. Correct it whenever it's wrong — that is the
point of the file.*

## Shared Windows CLI tools

On Joshua's Windows laptop, `uv`, `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`, `ffplay`, and `deno` are shared Windows user installs. Read `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\references\ai-workspace\windows-cli-toolchain.md` for their paths, versions, usage, and PATH refresh steps. Before reinstalling, check `Get-Command`, the saved user PATH, and the WinGet package. A running IDE may have stale PATH; refresh the shell or restart that IDE. Keep Python dependencies in project environments.

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Shared Windows CLI tools for Codex, Claude Code, and Antigravity | `references/ai-workspace/windows-cli-toolchain.md` — installed paths, PATH refresh, and usage for `uv`, `yt-dlp`, FFmpeg, and Deno |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| Current local work in Claude/Codex cloud | `setup/github-sync/README.md`; private `codex/local-sync/<machine>/<checkout>` branches and generated `.cloud-sync/` metadata/conversation exports. Cloud edits use separate task branches. |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — intake queue; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies clips into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized, so inbox presence alone does not mean unprocessed |
| Application code | `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `../ai-os-projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

Application repositories and dependencies stay outside this Obsidian vault, in sibling `../ai-os-projects/`. Do not create a junction from the vault back into that directory.

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **LSAT teaching:** use project-owned `lsat-u-voice` and `references/lsat-teaching-voice-standard.md`; this Joshua-selected standard takes precedence over older vendor voice blends.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Shared Windows command tools:** use `uv`, `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`, `ffplay`, and `deno` from the Windows user PATH. For versions, locations, how to invoke them, or a stale terminal environment, read `references/ai-workspace/windows-cli-toolchain.md`. Check before reinstalling; restart the affected IDE when its inherited PATH is stale.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.
- **Authorized cloud transfer (2026-10-07):** private sync branches also carry portable skills, memory and conversation exports. Joshua explicitly authorized credential transfer; implementation uses the authenticated encrypted package described in `setup/github-sync/README.md`; plaintext secrets and its unlock key stay outside GitHub and Drive. Cloud authentication and native browser sessions still require separate verification.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `transcript-intake` | retrieving, filing or reconciling student/session transcripts; separate from knowledge `ingest` |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"59152d","wall_time_seconds":1.0922964,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2222,"output":"# AGENTS.md\\r\\n\\r\\nThis file is the Codex-facing adapter for this repository. It summarizes the Claude-oriented project guidance from `CLAUDE.md` in a smaller format that is fast for Codex to scan. `CLAUDE.md` remains the fuller source of project knowledge.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Fast Locator\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`\\r\\n- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`\\r\\n- Live vault knowledge: `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/wiki/` (hubs, voice, business), `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/students/`, `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/corpus/`\\r\\n- Pre-2026-07-16 LSATU product knowledge (archived, read-only): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`\\r\\n\\r\\nIf an older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat it as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Project Map\\r\\n\\r\\n- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.\\r\\n- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.\\r\\n- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.\\r\\n- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.\\r\\n- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.\\r\\n- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.\\r\\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\\r\\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Stack And Commands\\r\\n\\r\\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\\r\\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\\r\\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\\r\\n- Build: `npm run build`\\r\\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\\r\\n- Test: `npm run test`\\r\\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\\r\\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\\r\\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Working Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\\r\\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\\r\\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\\r\\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\\r\\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\\r\\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\\r\\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\\r\\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product Constraints\\r\\n\\r\\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\\r\\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\\r\\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Smart Drill Note\\r\\n\\r\\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed \\"fix\\" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow Expectations\\r\\n\\r\\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\\r\\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\\r\\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\\r\\n\\r\\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\\r\\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong \\"Done\\" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Codex Adapter Layout\\r\\n\\r\\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\\r\\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\\r\\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known Gaps\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\\r\\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent skills\\r\\n\\r\\n### Issue tracker\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script running with cell ID 4\nWall time 31.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"9968f2","wall_time_seconds":1.25052,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":398,"output":"alter function public.activity_ai_source_v1(uuid,text,uuid,integer,text,integer) rename to activity_ai_source_base_v1;\\r\\nrevoke all on function public.activity_ai_source_base_v1(uuid,text,uuid,integer,text,integer) from public, anon, authenticated;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate function public.activity_ai_source_v1(p_owner uuid,p_kind text,p_source uuid,p_order integer,p_response text,p_attempt integer)\\r\\nreturns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path=pg_catalog,public as $$\\r\\ndeclare canonical jsonb; key jsonb;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n canonical:=public.activity_ai_source_base_v1(p_owner,p_kind,p_source,p_order,p_response,p_attempt);\\r\\n if canonical is null then return null; end if;\\r\\n if p_kind=\'assignment\' then\\r\\n  select v->\'answer_key\' into key from public.activity_assignments a, jsonb_array_elements(a.items) v\\r\\n   where a.id=p_source and a.student_id=p_owner and v->>\'order\'=p_order::text limit 1;\\r\\n else\\r\\n  select v->\'answer_key\' into key from public.activities a, jsonb_array_elements(a.items) v\\r\\n   where a.id=p_source and a.created_by=p_owner and v->>\'order\'=p_order::text limit 1;\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n if key is not null and key<>\'null\'::jsonb then\\r\\n  if jsonb_typeof(key) is distinct from \'object\' or jsonb_typeof(key->\'relationships\') is distinct from \'array\' or length(key::text)>16000 then return null; end if;\\r\\n  canonical:=canonical||jsonb_build_object(\'answerKey\',key);\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n if length(canonical::text)>64000 then return null; end if;\\r\\n return canonical;\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\nrevoke all on function public.activity_ai_source_v1(uuid,text,uuid,integer,text,integer) from public, anon, authenticated;\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script running with cell ID 4\nWall time 1.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"ffbf89","wall_time_seconds":5.6083373,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":6142,"output":"-- Additive, inactive Activity AI admission. No existing source records change.\\r\\nbegin;\\r\\ncreate table public.activity_ai_configuration_v1(\\r\\n singleton boolean primary key default true check(singleton), enabled boolean not null default false,\\r\\n actor_minute integer not null default 60 check(actor_minute between 1 and 600),\\r\\n actor_day integer not null default 600 check(actor_day between 1 and 6000),\\r\\n global_day integer not null default 6000 check(global_day between 1 and 60000),\\r\\n actor_concurrency integer not null default 3 check(actor_concurrency between 1 and 10),\\r\\n lease_seconds integer not null default 30 check(lease_seconds=30)\\r\\n);\\r\\ninsert into public.activity_ai_configuration_v1(singleton) values(true);\\r\\ncreate table public.activity_ai_receipts_v1(\\r\\n id uuid primary key, owner_id uuid not null, source_kind text not null check(source_kind in (\'assignment\',\'preview\')),\\r\\n source_id uuid not null, item_order integer not null check(item_order>0), attempt_no integer not null check(attempt_no between 1 and 2),\\r\\n canonical_payload jsonb not null check(jsonb_typeof(canonical_payload)=\'object\'),\\r\\n payload_sha256 text not null check(payload_sha256 ~ \'^[0-9a-f]{64}$\'),\\r\\n state text not null check(state in (\'reserved\',\'dispatched\',\'completed\',\'failed\',\'uncertain\')),\\r\\n claim_id uuid not null, lease_expires_at timestamptz not null,\\r\\n result jsonb, failure_category text check(failure_category in (\'provider_error\',\'timeout\',\'malformed_result\',\'transport_error\',\'cancelled\',\'access_revoked\',\'source_changed\',\'uncertain\')),\\r\\n admitted_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(), dispatched_at timestamptz, finished_at timestamptz,\\r\\n unique(owner_id,payload_sha256),unique(id,owner_id),\\r\\n check((state=\'completed\')=(result is not null)), check(state<>\'completed\' or failure_category is null)\\r\\n);\\r\\ncreate table public.activity_ai_request_keys_v1(\\r\\n request_id uuid primary key, owner_id uuid not null, receipt_id uuid not null,\\r\\n payload_sha256 text not null, created_at timestamptz not null default clock_timestamp(),\\r\\n foreign key(receipt_id,owner_id) references public.activity_ai_receipts_v1(id,owner_id)\\r\\n);\\r\\ncreate table public.activity_ai_budget_buckets_v1(\\r\\n kind text not null check(kind in (\'actor-minute\',\'actor-day\',\'global-day\')), actor_key text not null,\\r\\n bucket_start timestamptz not null, admissions integer not null check(admissions>=0),primary key(kind,actor_key,bucket_start)\\r\\n);\\r\\ncreate index activity_ai_owner_pending_v1 on public.activity_ai_receipts_v1(owner_id,state,lease_expires_at);\\r\\nalter table public.activity_ai_configuration_v1 enable row level security;\\r\\nalter table public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 enable row level security;\\r\\nalter table public.activity_ai_request_keys_v1 enable row level security;\\r\\nalter table public.activity_ai_budget_buckets_v1 enable row level security;\\r\\nrevoke all on public.activity_ai_configuration_v1,public.activity_ai_receipts_v1,public.activity_ai_request_keys_v1,public.activity_ai_budget_buckets_v1 from public,anon,authenticated,service_role;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Source locks synchronize archive, assignment revocation and admin edits with\\r\\n-- admission/dispatch. Missing deployed revocation/archive columns fail closed.\\r\\ncreate function public.activity_ai_source_v1(p_owner uuid,p_kind text,p_source uuid,p_order integer,p_response text,p_attempt integer)\\r\\nreturns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path=pg_catalog,public as $$\\r\\ndeclare profile jsonb; source jsonb; item jsonb; matches integer; canonical jsonb; first_confirmed boolean; trusted_ideas jsonb; trusted_coach jsonb;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n select to_jsonb(p) into profile from public.profiles p where p.class_id=p_owner::text for share;\\r\\n if profile is null or not profile ? \'archived_at\' or profile->>\'archived_at\' is not null then return null; end if;\\r\\n if p_kind=\'assignment\' then\\r\\n  select to_jsonb(a) into source from public.activity_assignments a where a.id=p_source and a.student_id=p_owner for share;\\r\\n  if source is null or not source ? \'revoked_at\' or source->>\'revoked_at\' is not null\\r\\n    or source->>\'status\' is null or source->>\'status\' not in (\'assigned\',\'in_progress\',\'completed\') then return null; end if;\\r\\n elsif p_kind=\'preview\' then\\r\\n  perform 1 from public.user_roles where user_id=p_owner and role::text=\'admin\' for share;\\r\\n  if not found then return null; end if;\\r\\n  select to_jsonb(a) into source from public.activities a where a.id=p_source and a.created_by=p_owner for share;\\r\\n  if source is null then return null; end if;\\r\\n else return null; end if;\\r\\n if source->>\'item_format\' is null or source->>\'item_format\' not in (\'free-text\',\'variation\',\'mcq\') or jsonb_typeof(source->\'items\') is distinct from \'array\' then return null; end if;\\r\\n trusted_coach:=source->\'instructions\'->\'coach\';\\r\\n -- Only absent/null briefs may use the original legacy negation rubric.\\r\\n -- A supplied malformed brief must never silently change the skill graded.\\r\\n if trusted_coach is not null and trusted_coach<>\'null\'::jsonb then\\r\\n  if jsonb_typeof(trusted_coach) is distinct from \'object\'\\r\\n    or jsonb_typeof(trusted_coach->\'drill\') is distinct from \'string\'\\r\\n    or length(btrim(trusted_coach->>\'drill\'))<1 or length(trusted_coach->>\'drill\')>1000\\r\\n    or jsonb_typeof(trusted_coach->\'rubric\') is distinct from \'array\' then return null; end if;\\r\\n  if jsonb_array_length(trusted_coach->\'rubric\') not between 1 and 50\\r\\n    or exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(trusted_coach->\'rubric\') v where jsonb_typeof(v) is distinct from \'string\'\\r\\n      or length(btrim(v#>>\'{}\'))<1 or length(v#>>\'{}\')>4000)\\r\\n    or (trusted_coach ? \'partial_rule\' and (jsonb_typeof(trusted_coach->\'partial_rule\') is distinct from \'string\'\\r\\n      or length(trusted_coach->>\'partial_rule\')>4000))\\r\\n    or length(trusted_coach::text)>24000 then return null; end if;\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n if exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(source->\'items\') v where jsonb_typeof(v) is distinct from \'object\'\\r\\n   or jsonb_typeof(v->\'order\') is distinct from \'number\' or coalesce(v->>\'order\',\'\') !~ \'^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$\') then return null; end if;\\r\\n select count(*) into matches from jsonb_array_elements(source->\'items\') v where v->>\'order\'=p_order::text;\\r\\n if matches<>1 then return null; end if;\\r\\n select v into item from jsonb_array_elements(source->\'items\') v where v->>\'order\'=p_order::text;\\r\\n if jsonb_typeof(item->\'prompt\') is distinct from \'string\' or length(btrim(item->>\'prompt\'))<1 or length(item->>\'prompt\')>12000\\r\\n  or jsonb_typeof(item->\'credited_answers\') is distinct from \'array\' or jsonb_array_length(item->\'credited_answers\') not between 1 and 20\\r\\n  or exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(item->\'credited_answers\') v where jsonb_typeof(v)<>\'string\' or length(btrim(v#>>\'{}\'))<1 or length(v#>>\'{}\')>4000)\\r\\n  or length((item->\'credited_answers\')::text)>20000 or length(coalesce(item->>\'explanation\',\'\'))>12000\\r\\n  or length(coalesce(source->\'instructions\'->\'coach\',\'{}\'::jsonb)::text)>24000 then return null; end if;\\r\\n first_confirmed:=false;\\r\\n if p_attempt=2 then\\r\\n  select exists(select 1 from public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 r where r.owner_id=p_owner and r.source_kind=p_kind\\r\\n   and r.source_id=p_source and r.item_order=p_order and r.attempt_no=1 and r.state=\'completed\'\\r\\n   and r.canonical_payload->>\'statement\' is not distinct from item->>\'prompt\'\\r\\n   and r.canonical_payload->\'creditedAnswers\' is not distinct from item->\'credited_answers\'\\r\\n   and r.canonical_payload->>\'explanation\' is not distinct from coalesce(item->>\'explanation\',\'\')\\r\\n   and r.canonical_payload->>\'category\' is not distinct from item->>\'category\'\\r\\n   and r.canonical_payload->\'coach\' is not distinct from coalesce(source->\'instructions\'->\'coach\',\'null\'::jsonb)\\r\\n   and (p_kind=\'preview\' or exists(select 1 from public.activity_item_responses a where a.assignment_id=p_source and a.student_id=p_owner\\r\\n     and a.item_order=p_order and a.attempt_no=1 and a.response_text=r.canonical_payload->>\'studentResponse\' and a.verdict=r.result->>\'verdict\')))\\r\\n  into first_confirmed;\\r\\n  if not first_confirmed then return null; end if;\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n canonical:=jsonb_build_object(\'statement\',item->>\'prompt\',\'creditedAnswers\',item->\'credited_answers\',\\r\\n  \'explanation\',coalesce(item->>\'explanation\',\'\'),\'category\',item->>\'category\',\'coach\',source->\'instructions\'->\'coach\',\\r\\n  \'studentResponse\',p_response,\'attemptNo\',p_attempt,\'sourceKind\',p_kind,\'sourceId\',p_source,\'itemOrder\',p_order,\\r\\n  \'firstAttemptConfirmed\',first_confirmed);\\r\\n -- Press checklists are authored in the trusted explanation (same numbered\\r\\n -- separators as parsePress). Never accept an independent client checklist.\\r\\n trusted_ideas:=item->\'ideas\';\\r\\n if trusted_ideas is null and position(\'the big ideas\' in lower(coalesce(item->>\'explanation\',\'\')))>0 then\\r\\n  select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(\'n\',m[1]::integer,\'text\',regexp_replace(btrim(m[2]),\'[[:space:]]*[.]$\',\'\')) order by m[1]::integer)\\r\\n   into trusted_ideas from regexp_matches(regexp_replace(item->>\'explanation\',\'[.][[:space:]]*Named traps:.*$\',\'\',\'i\'),\'[(]([0-9])[)][[:space:]]*([^·]+?)(?=[[:space:]]*·|$)\',\'g\') m;\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n if trusted_ideas is not null then\\r\\n  if jsonb_typeof(trusted_ideas) is distinct from \'array\' or jsonb_array_length(trusted_ideas) not between 1 and 100\\r\\n   or exists(select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(trusted_ideas) idea where jsonb_typeof(idea) is distinct from \'object\'\\r\\n     or jsonb_typeof(idea->\'n\') is distinct from \'number\' or coalesce(idea->>\'n\',\'\') !~ \'^[1-9][0-9]{0,5}$\' or jsonb_typeof(idea->\'text\') is distinct from \'string\'\\r\\n     or length(btrim(idea->>\'text\'))<1 or length(idea->>\'text\')>2000)\\r\\n   or (select count(*) from jsonb_array_elements(trusted_ideas))<>(select count(distinct idea->>\'n\') from jsonb_array_elements(trusted_ideas) idea) then return null; end if;\\r\\n  canonical:=canonical||jsonb_build_object(\'ideas\',trusted_ideas);\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n if length(canonical::text)>64000 then return null; end if;\\r\\n return canonical;\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate function public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request uuid,p_receipt public.activity_ai_receipts_v1,p_status text,p_http integer,p_code text)\\r\\nreturns jsonb language sql set search_path=pg_catalog,public as $$\\r\\n select jsonb_strip_nulls(jsonb_build_object(\'ok\',p_status in (\'reserved\',\'cached\',\'dispatched\',\'completed\'),\\r\\n  \'status\',p_status,\'httpStatus\',p_http,\'code\',p_code,\'requestId\',p_request,\'receiptId\',p_receipt.id,\\r\\n  \'claimId\',case when p_status=\'reserved\' then p_receipt.claim_id end,\\r\\n  \'leaseExpiresAt\',case when p_status in (\'reserved\',\'in_flight\') then p_receipt.lease_expires_at end,\\r\\n  \'payload\',case when p_status in (\'reserved\',\'dispatched\') then p_receipt.canonical_payload end,\\r\\n  \'result\',case when p_status in (\'cached\',\'completed\') then p_receipt.result end,\\r\\n  \'retryAfterSeconds\',case when p_status=\'in_flight\' then greatest(1,ceil(extract(epoch from p_receipt.lease_expires_at-clock_timestamp())))::integer end));\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate function public.begin_activity_ai_request_v1(p_owner_id uuid,p_request_id uuid,p_source_kind text,p_source_id uuid,p_item_order integer,p_student_response text,p_attempt_no integer)\\r\\nreturns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path=pg_catalog,public as $$\\r\\ndeclare r public.activity_ai_receipts_v1; k public.activity_ai_request_keys_v1; c public.activity_ai_configuration_v1;\\r\\n payload jsonb; digest text; at_time timestamptz; minute_start timestamptz; day_start timestamptz; active_count integer;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n if p_owner_id is null or p_request_id is null or p_source_id is null or p_item_order is null or p_item_order<1\\r\\n  or p_source_kind is null or p_source_kind not in (\'assignment\',\'preview\') or p_attempt_no is null or p_attempt_no not between 1 and 2\\r\\n  or p_student_response is null or length(btrim(p_student_response))<1 or length(p_student_response)>10000 or octet_length(p_student_response)>40000 then\\r\\n  return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',422,\'invalid_request\'); end if;\\r\\n perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(\'activity-ai-global-v1\',0));\\r\\n perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(\'activity-ai-owner-v1:\'||p_owner_id::text,0));\\r\\n payload:=public.activity_ai_source_v1(p_owner_id,p_source_kind,p_source_id,p_item_order,p_student_response,p_attempt_no);\\r\\n if payload is null then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',403,\'source_unavailable\'); end if;\\r\\n digest:=encode(sha256(convert_to(payload::text,\'UTF8\')),\'hex\');\\r\\n select * into k from public.activity_ai_request_keys_v1 where request_id=p_request_id;\\r\\n if found then\\r\\n  if k.owner_id<>p_owner_id or k.payload_sha256<>digest then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',409,\'request_conflict\'); end if;\\r\\n  select * into r from public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 where id=k.receipt_id for update;\\r\\n else\\r\\n  select * into r from public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 where owner_id=p_owner_id and payload_sha256=digest for update;\\r\\n  if found then insert into public.activity_ai_request_keys_v1 values(p_request_id,p_owner_id,r.id,digest,clock_timestamp()); end if;\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n if r.id is not null and r.canonical_payload<>payload then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',409,\'request_conflict\'); end if;\\r\\n if r.state=\'completed\' then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'cached\',200,\'cached\'); end if;\\r\\n if r.state=\'failed\' then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'failed\',502,\'request_failed\'); end if;\\r\\n at_time:=clock_timestamp();\\r\\n if r.state in (\'dispatched\',\'uncertain\') then\\r\\n  if r.state=\'dispatched\' and r.lease_expires_at<=at_time then update public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 set state=\'uncertain\' where id=r.id returning * into r; end if;\\r\\n  return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,case when r.state=\'uncertain\' then \'uncertain\' else \'in_flight\' end,409,case when r.state=\'uncertain\' then \'dispatch_uncertain\' else \'request_in_flight\' end);\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n if r.state=\'reserved\' and r.lease_expires_at>at_time then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'in_flight\',409,\'request_in_flight\'); end if;\\r\\n select * into c from public.activity_ai_configuration_v1 where singleton for share;\\r\\n if not coalesce(c.enabled,false) then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',503,\'contract_disabled\'); end if;\\r\\n -- Unknown dispatched outcomes count against concurrency until explicitly\\r\\n -- finished by the original claim; they never expire into an automatic retry.\\r\\n update public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 set state=\'uncertain\' where owner_id=p_owner_id and state=\'dispatched\' and lease_expires_at<=at_time;\\r\\n select count(*) into active_count from public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 where owner_id=p_owner_id\\r\\n  and (state in (\'dispatched\',\'uncertain\') or (state=\'reserved\' and lease_expires_at>at_time));\\r\\n if active_count>=c.actor_concurrency then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',429,\'concurrency_limited\'); end if;\\r\\n if r.id is not null then\\r\\n  update public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 set claim_id=gen_random_uuid(),lease_expires_at=at_time+interval \'30 seconds\' where id=r.id returning * into r;\\r\\n  return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'reserved\',200,\'reserved\');\\r\\n end if;\\r\\n minute_start:=date_trunc(\'minute\',at_time at time zone \'UTC\') at time zone \'UTC\';\\r\\n day_start:=date_trunc(\'day\',at_time at time zone \'UTC\') at time zone \'UTC\';\\r\\n if coalesce((select admissions from public.activity_ai_budget_buckets_v1 where kind=\'actor-minute\' and actor_key=p_owner_id::text and bucket_start=minute_start),0)>=c.actor_minute\\r\\n  or coalesce((select admissions from public.activity_ai_budget_buckets_v1 where kind=\'actor-day\' and actor_key=p_owner_id::text and bucket_start=day_start),0)>=c.actor_day\\r\\n  or coalesce((select admissions from public.activity_ai_budget_buckets_v1 where kind=\'global-day\' and actor_key=\'global\' and bucket_start=day_start),0)>=c.global_day then\\r\\n  return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',429,\'rate_limited\'); end if;\\r\\n insert into public.activity_ai_budget_buckets_v1 values(\'actor-minute\',p_owner_id::text,minute_start,1),(\'actor-day\',p_owner_id::text,day_start,1),(\'global-day\',\'global\',day_start,1)\\r\\n on conflict(kind,actor_key,bucket_start) do update set admissions=activity_ai_budget_buckets_v1.admissions+1;\\r\\n insert into public.activity_ai_receipts_v1(id,owner_id,source_kind,source_id,item_order,attempt_no,canonical_payload,payload_sha256,state,claim_id,lease_expires_at,admitted_at)\\r\\n values(p_request_id,p_owner_id,p_source_kind,p_source_id,p_item_order,p_attempt_no,payload,digest,\'reserved\',gen_random_uuid(),at_time+interval \'30 seconds\',at_time) returning * into r;\\r\\n insert into public.activity_ai_request_keys_v1 values(p_request_id,p_owner_id,r.id,digest,at_time);\\r\\n return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'reserved\',200,\'reserved\');\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate function public.dispatch_activity_ai_request_v1(p_owner_id uuid,p_request_id uuid,p_claim_id uuid)\\r\\nreturns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path=pg_catalog,public as $$\\r\\ndeclare r public.activity_ai_receipts_v1; payload jsonb; c public.activity_ai_configuration_v1;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(\'activity-ai-global-v1\',0));\\r\\n perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(\'activity-ai-owner-v1:\'||coalesce(p_owner_id::text,\'\'),0));\\r\\n select r1.* into r from public.activity_ai_request_keys_v1 k join public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 r1 on r1.id=k.receipt_id\\r\\n where k.request_id=p_request_id and k.owner_id=p_owner_id for update of r1;\\r\\n if r.id is null or p_claim_id is null or r.claim_id<>p_claim_id then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',409,\'claim_conflict\'); end if;\\r\\n payload:=public.activity_ai_source_v1(r.owner_id,r.source_kind,r.source_id,r.item_order,r.canonical_payload->>\'studentResponse\',r.attempt_no);\\r\\n if payload is null then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',403,\'source_unavailable\'); end if;\\r\\n if payload<>r.canonical_payload then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',409,\'source_changed\'); end if;\\r\\n if r.state=\'completed\' then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'cached\',200,\'cached\'); end if;\\r\\n if r.state in (\'dispatched\',\'uncertain\') then\\r\\n  if r.state=\'dispatched\' and r.lease_expires_at<=clock_timestamp() then update public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 set state=\'uncertain\' where id=r.id returning * into r; end if;\\r\\n  return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,case when r.state=\'uncertain\' then \'uncertain\' else \'in_flight\' end,409,\'dispatch_not_repeatable\'); end if;\\r\\n if r.state<>\'reserved\' or r.lease_expires_at<=clock_timestamp() then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',409,\'claim_expired\'); end if;\\r\\n select * into c from public.activity_ai_configuration_v1 where singleton for share;\\r\\n if not coalesce(c.enabled,false) then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',503,\'contract_disabled\'); end if;\\r\\n update public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 set state=\'dispatched\',dispatched_at=clock_timestamp(),lease_expires_at=clock_timestamp()+interval \'30 seconds\'\\r\\n where id=r.id and state=\'reserved\' and claim_id=p_claim_id returning * into r;\\r\\n return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'dispatched\',200,\'dispatched\');\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\n\\r\\ncreate function public.finish_activity_ai_request_v1(p_owner_id uuid,p_request_id uuid,p_claim_id uuid,p_result jsonb default null,p_failure_category text default null)\\r\\nreturns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path=pg_catalog,public as $$\\r\\ndeclare r public.activity_ai_receipts_v1; end if;\\r\\n if r.state=\'completed\' then\\r\\n  payload:=public.activity_ai_source_v1(r.owner_id,r.source_kind,r.source_id,r.item_order,r.canonical_payload->>\'studentResponse\',r.attempt_no);\\r\\n  if payload is null then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',403,\'source_unavailable\'); end if;\\r\\n  if payload<>r.canonical_payload then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',409,\'source_changed\'); end if;\\r\\n  if p_failure_category is null and p_result=r.result then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'cached\',200,\'cached\'); end if;\\r\\n  return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',409,\'finish_conflict\'); end if;\\r\\n if r.state=\'failed\' then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'failed\',502,\'request_failed\'); end if;\\r\\n if r.state not in (\'dispatched\',\'uncertain\') then return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',409,\'not_dispatched\'); end if;\\r\\n payload:=public.activity_ai_source_v1(r.owner_id,r.source_kind,r.source_id,r.item_order,r.canonical_payload->>\'studentResponse\',r.attempt_no);\\r\\n if payload is null then p_result:=null;p_failure_category:=\'access_revoked\';\\r\\n elsif payload<>r.canonical_payload then p_result:=null;p_failure_category:=\'source_changed\'; end if;\\r\\n if p_failure_category is not null then\\r\\n  if p_result is not null or p_failure_category not in (\'provider_error\',\'timeout\',\'malformed_result\',\'transport_error\',\'cancelled\',\'access_revoked\',\'source_changed\',\'uncertain\') then\\r\\n   return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',422,\'invalid_finish\'); end if;\\r\\n  update public.activity_ai_receipts_v1 set state=case when p_failure_category=\'uncertain\' then \'uncertain\' else \'failed\' end,\\r\\n   failure_category=p_failure_category,finished_at=case when p_failure_category=\'uncertain\' then null else clock_timestamp() end where id=r.id returning * into r;\\r\\n  return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,case when r.state=\'uncertain\' then \'uncertain\' else \'failed\' end,502,case when r.state=\'uncertain\' then \'dispatch_uncertain\' else \'request_failed\' end); end if;\\r\\n if jsonb_typeof(p_result) is distinct from \'object\' or p_result->>\'verdict\' is null or p_result->>\'verdict\' not in (\'correct\',\'partial\',\'incorrect\')\\r\\n  or jsonb_typeof(p_result->\'feedback\') is distinct from \'string\' or length(btrim(p_result->>\'feedback\'))<1 or length(p_result->>\'feedback\')>2000\\r\\n  or exists(select 1 from jsonb_object_keys(p_result) key where key not in (\'verdict\',\'feedback\',\'covered\')) then\\r\\n  return public.activity_ai_reply_v1(p_request_id,r,\'denied\',422,\'invalid_result\'); [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 15.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"585eab","wall_time_seconds":0.9469053,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":11,"output":"312b62bea08981d05f708f0781029a92158b2a41\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"970879","wall_time_seconds":1.1296556,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1432,"output":"   * A named-but-absent type draws as a ghost that dissolves.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  drawn: RelationKind[];\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The words a student uses to name each type. The drill\'s own dial says\\r\\n * \\"Comparison\\", so that has to count; so do the textbook names a student brings\\r\\n * from elsewhere (\\"necessary condition\\", \\"if-then\\", \\"cause and effect\\").\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst KIND_WORDS: [RegExp, RelationKind][] = [\\r\\n  [\\r\\n    /\\\\bconditional(?:s|ly)?\\\\b|\\\\b(?:necessary|sufficient)\\\\s+condition\\\\b|\\\\bif\\\\s*[-/]?\\\\s*then\\\\b|\\\\brequirement\\\\b/gi,\\r\\n    \\"conditional\\",\\r\\n  ],\\r\\n  [/\\\\bcausal(?:ity|ly)?\\\\b|\\\\bcausation\\\\b|\\\\bcause[sd]?\\\\b/gi, \\"causal\\"],\\r\\n  [/\\\\bcomparat(?:ive|ively)\\\\b|\\\\bcomparisons?\\\\b|\\\\bcompar(?:e|es|ing|ed)\\\\b/gi, \\"comparative\\"],\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * \\"Conditional, requires (not causal)\\" rules causal OUT. Counting that as a\\r\\n * claim of causation drew a ghost for the exact mistake the student had just\\r\\n * avoided, so a type word sitting right after a negation is not a claim.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst NEGATED_BEFORE = /\\\\b(?:not|no|never|isn\'t|isnt|is\\\\s+not|rather\\\\s+than|instead\\\\s+of|neither|nor)\\\\s+(?:a\\\\s+|an\\\\s+|the\\\\s+)?$/i;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction namedKinds(response: string): Set<RelationKind> {\\r\\n  const named = new Set<RelationKind>();\\r\\n  for (const [re, kind] of KIND_WORDS) {\\r\\n    for (const m of response.matchAll(re)) {\\r\\n      const before = response.slice(Math.max(0, (m.index ?? 0) - 24), m.index);\\r\\n      if (!NEGATED_BEFORE.test(before)) named.add(kind);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return named;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Read a free-text answer in the drill\'s game format (\\"conditional, only if\\").\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The type is what gets matched; the indicator is checked separately, because\\r\\n * the drill\'s own rubric says type alone is not a complete answer and the\\r\\n * world should be able to show a figure drawn from the wrong anchor words.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function readAnswer(signal: Signal, response: string): Reading {\\r\\n  const named = namedKinds(response);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const real = new Set(signal.relations.map((r) => r.kind));\\r\\n\\r\\n  const found = [...named].filter((k) => real.has(k));\\r\\n  const invented = [...named].filter((k) => !real.has(k));\\r\\n  const drawn = matchedKinds(signal, response);\\r\\n  const missed = [...real].filter((k) => !drawn.includes(k));\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Did they quote an actual indicator for each type they got right?\\r\\n  const quotedIndicators = found.length > 0 && found.every((kind) => drawn.includes(kind));\\r\\n\\r\\n  return { found, invented, missed, quotedIndicators, drawn };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *\\r\\n * Instant grading\\r\\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Lowercase, straight quotes gone, \\"only-if\\" read as \\"only if\\". */\\r\\nfunction normalize(text: string): string {\\r\\n  return text\\r\\n    .toLowerCase()\\r\\n    .replace(/[‘’“”\\"\'`]/g, \\" \\")\\r\\n    .replace(/[-_/]/g, \\" \\")\\r\\n    .replace(/\\\\s+/g, \\" \\");\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Words that turn a short indicator into a different one. \\"Only if\\" is not\\r\\n * \\"if\\", and a student who writes \\"only if\\" on an item keyed \\"if\\" has named the\\r\\n * wrong condition, so a bare \\"if\\" match inside \\"only if\\" proves nothing.\\r\\n */\\r\\nconst LONGER_FORM = /\\\\b(?:only|even|as|not)\\\\s+$/;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction quotes(indicator: string, answer: string): boolean {\\r\\n  const parts = normalize(indicator.replace(/[.,]+$/, \\"\\"))\\r\\n    .split(/…|\\\\.\\\\.\\\\./)\\r\\n    .map((p) => p.trim())\\r\\n    .filter(Boolean);\\r\\n  if (!parts.length) return false;\\r\\n  return parts.every((part) => {\\r\\n    const re = new RegExp(`\\\\\\\\b${escape(part)}\\\\\\\\b`, \\"g\\");\\r\\n    for (const m of answer.matchAll(re)) {\\r\\n      if (!LONGER_FORM.test(answer.slice(0, m.index))) return true;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return false;\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * True when an answer is the only ever says yes. A bag of words cannot distinguish\\r\\n  // \\"causal, since; comparative, more evenly than\\" from the swapped labels.\\r\\n  // Unstructured prose stays with the Coach rather than being guessed correct.\\r\\n  return response.split(/[;\\\\n]+|,\\\\s*(?=(?:conditional|causal|causation|comparative|comparison|necessary condition|sufficient condition)\\\\b)/i).filter((part) => part.trim()).map((part) => ({\\r\\n    named: namedKinds(part),\\r\\n    answer: normalize(part),\\r\\n  }));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction matchedKinds(signal: Signal, response: string): RelationKind[] {\\r\\n  const pairs = answerPairs(response);\\r\\n  return signal.relations.filter((r) => {\\r\\n    const claimed = pairs.filter((pair) => pair.named.has(r.kind));\\r\\n    return claimed.length > 0 && claimed.every((pair) =>\\r\\n      pair.named.size === 1 && r.indicators.some((indicator) => quotes(indicator, pair.answer)),\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }).map((r) => r.kind);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function matchesCredited(item: ActivityItem, response: string): boolean {\\r\\n  const signal = parseSignal(item);\\r\\n  if (!signal || !response.trim()) return false;\\r\\n  const named = namedKinds(response);\\r\\n  const real = new Set(signal.relations.map((r) => r.kind));\\r\\n  if (named.size !== real.size || [...named].some((k) => !real.has(k))) return false;\\r\\n  if (!answerPairs(response).every((pair) => pair.named.size === 1 && signal.relations.some((r) =>\\r\\n    pair.named.has(r.kind) && r.indicators.some((indicator) => quotes(indicator, pair.answer)),\\r\\n  ))) return false;\\r\\n  const matched = matchedKinds(signal, response);\\r\\n  return signal.relations.every((r) => matched.includes(r.kind));\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"3dc62a","wall_time_seconds":1.3802106,"exit_code":1,"original_token_count":2289,"output":"rg: drizzle/migrations/002*: The filename, directory name, or volume label syntax is incorrect. expect(r.connects.trim()).not.toBe(\\"\\");\\r\\n        expect(r.why.trim()).not.toBe(\\"\\");\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      expect(Array.isArray(key.traps)).toBe(true);\\r\\n      // The key pairs exactly what the reviewed credited answer pairs.\\r\\n      expect(correct(i)).toBe(i.credited_answers[0]);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"separated comparatives record both required halves\\", () expect(comp.required_parts?.[0].length).toBe(2);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  it(\\"contextual comparisons carry their benchmark\\", () => {\\r\\n    for (const n of [10, 29, 35, 36, 47]) content.instructions.coach.grading_rubric as string[];\\r\\n    expect(rubric.join(\\" \\")).toMatch(/Partial/);\\r\\n    expect(rubric.join(\\" \\")).toMatch(/both halves/);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n});\\r\\n\\r\\ndescribe(\\"instant grading stays confident-only\\", () => {\\r\\n  it.each(items.map((i) => [i.order, i]))(\\"credits the keyed answer for item %s instantly\\", (_n, i) => \'conditional, \\"only if\\"; causal, \\"because\\"; comparative, \\"four degrees\\"\'],\\r\\n    [\\"swapped pair\\", \'causal, \\"only if\\"; conditional, \\"because\\"\'],\\r\\n  ])(\\"sends a %s expect(matchesCredited(byOrder(43), \'causal, \\"lowers\\"; comparative, \\"faster\\"\')).toBe(false);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n});\\r\\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \\"@/integrations/supabase/client\\";\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \\"@/contexts/AuthContext\\";\\r\\nimport type { ActivityItem, CoachVerdict } from number; source?: CoachSource }\\r\\nexport interface JudgeResult { ok: boolean; verdict?: CoachVerdict; error?: string; discarded?: boolean }\\r\\nconst COACH_TIMEOUT_MS = 12_000;\\r\\nexport function useActivityCoach() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const actor = user?.id;\\r\\n  const scope = useRef(actor); scope.current = actor;\\r\\n  const mounted = useRef(true);\\r\\n  useEffect(() => { mounted.current = true; return () => { mounted.current = false; }; }, []);\\r\\n  const [busyActor, setBusyActor] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const judge = Its saved identity is kept for retry.\\" };\\r\\n      if (!data || ![\\"correct\\", \\"partial\\", \\"incorrect\\"].includes(data.verdict) || typeof data.feedback !== \\"string\\" || !data.feedback.trim()) return { ok: false, error: \\"Coach returned no verified judgment.\\" };\\r\\n      const covered = Array.isArray(data.covered) ? data.covered.filter((n: unknown) => typeof n === \\"number\\" && Number.isFinite(n)) : undefined;\\r\\n      return { ok: true, verdict: { verdict: data.verdict, feedback: data.feedback, covered } };\\r\\n    } catch {\\r\\n      if (!current()) return { ok: false, discarded: true };\\r\\n      return { ok: false, error: abort.signal.aborted ? \\"Coach timed out. Its saved request identity is kept for retry.\\" : \\"Coach unavailable. Its saved request identity is kept for retry.\\" };\\r\\n    } finally { clearTimeout(timeout); if (current()) setBusyActor(null); }\\r\\n  }, [actor]);\\r\\n  return { judge, judging: Boolean(actor && busyActor === actor) };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]
